sid2024.image=web_promo_small/spromo_XXXXXXX.png
sid2024.text=Learning more about Cyberbullying\n\nStrap in for a wild ride through the virtual cosmos as you embark on a mission to rescue the internet from the clutches of one notorious villain: the malevolent Deepfake. It's time to don your digital superhero cape, unleash your meme-tastic powers, and join forces with a squad of cyber-savvy comrades to safeguard the online realm. Get ready to LOL in the face of danger, ROFL your way through challenges, and GIF your all to save the day. \nIn this pixelated battleground, where memes clash and GIFs dance, your ultimate weapon against the forces of Deepfake is not just a joystick or a keyboard - it's knowledge! To emerge victorious you'll need a brain as sharp as a cyber katana. Arm yourself with insights on cyberbullying, outsmart the trolls with your wisdom, and decode the secrets of digital empathy. Whether you're a seasoned cyber warrior or a novice in the realm of online etiquette, the only way to conquer this game is by embracing the power of knowledge about cyberbullying. So, brush up on your digital ethics, wield your intellect like a magic wand, and let the battle for a safer internet begin!\n\nTo help you in your journey, here are some facts about cyberbullying:\n\n*Harassment* - Is sustained and intentional Bullying composed of abusive or threatening messages that can severely affect a person's mental well-being.\n*Dissing* - Spreading cruel information to ruin someone's reputation or relationships. Usually the bully knows or has a personal relationship with the victim.\n*Outing or Doxing* - Is the act of revealing sensitive or personal information (messages, photos, videos, etc) about someone without their consent, aiming to cause them harm or humiliation.\n*Cyberstalking* - The use of technology (most often, the internet) to make someone else afraid or concerned about their safety. This is already a crime in a lot of countries worldwide and can have tragic consequences.\n*Exclusion* - Deliberately excluding an individual from online activities, groups, or conversations to isolate and ostracize them.\n*Impersonation* - Pretending to be someone else online with the intent to deceive or harm their reputation.\n*Fake profiles* - Usually created to hide the attacker's real identity with the intention to bully others without revealing who they are.\n*Frapping* - It happens when someone steals your account and starts to impersonate you online posting inappropriate content. A fake profile can also be used for the same thing.\n*Trolling* - Trolling is when a person intentionally tries to incite negative reactions by posting inflammatory comments online. Trolling bullies tend to be more interested in creating conflict and don't have a personal relationship with their victims.\n*Flaming* - Intentionally posting false, harmful, or defamatory information about someone with the aim of damaging their reputation.\n*Cyberbullying by Proxy* - Enlisting others to engage in cyberbullying on behalf of the instigator, often without the target's knowledge.\n*Catfishing* - Happens when online identities are stolen to recreate social networking profiles for deceptive purposes. Happens when online identities are stolen for deceptive purposes.\n*Trickery* - Involves gaining the victim's trust so that secrets can be shared publicly online later.\nHow to play:\n\n- Answer each question correctly to deffeat Deepfake and save the internet.\n\n Big thanks to our builders for helping us create the games!\n\n*Create, connect and share respect! Remember a better internet starts with you.*
